```go
func TestARoleCannotBeGrantedAboveTheGrantersHighest(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	api.NewRoles(e.Repo, e.Clk, "dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)

	// The owner makes a "staff" role at position 10 that may manage roles, and
	// an "admin" role at position 50 that may not be handed out by staff.
	staff := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "staff", 10, api.PermViewChannel|api.PermManageRoles)
	high := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "high", 50, api.PermManageMessages)

	member, memberTok := e.NewUser("staffer")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, memberTok)
	grant(t, e, cid, ownerTok, member, staff, http.StatusNoContent)

	// The staffer may grant a role below their own highest…
	low := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "low", 5, api.PermAddReactions)
	victim, victimTok := e.NewUser("victim")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, victimTok)
	grant(t, e, cid, memberTok, victim, low, http.StatusNoContent)

	// …but not one at or above it.
	grant(t, e, cid, memberTok, victim, high, http.StatusForbidden)
	grant(t, e, cid, memberTok, victim, staff, http.StatusForbidden)
}

func TestOverwriteRoutesRoundTrip(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	api.NewRoles(e.Repo, e.Clk, "dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	ch, _, _ := newChannel(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 0, 1, 0, "general")
	member, memberTok := e.NewUser("member")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, memberTok)

	path := "/v1/channels/" + ch.String() + "/overwrites/1/" + member.String()
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPut, path, ownerTok,
		[]any{uint64(0), uint64(api.PermSendMessages)}); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("PUT overwrite failed")
	}
	ow, err := e.Repo.ListOverwrites(t.Context(), ch)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ListOverwrites: %v", err)
	}
	if len(ow) != 1 || ow[0].TargetKind != 1 || ow[0].TargetID != member ||
		ow[0].Deny != uint64(api.PermSendMessages) {
		t.Fatalf("overwrite = %+v", ow)
	}

	got, err := api.NewResolver(e.Repo).Resolve(t.Context(), member, mustChannel(t, e, ch))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Resolve: %v", err)
	}
	if got.Has(api.PermSendMessages) {
		t.Fatal("the deny overwrite did not take effect")
	}

	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodDelete, path, ownerTok, nil); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("DELETE overwrite failed")
	}
	if ow, _ := e.Repo.ListOverwrites(t.Context(), ch); len(ow) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("overwrite survived the delete: %+v", ow)
	}
}

// The two escalation paths a position bound alone does not close: a staffer who
// may manage roles must not be able to MINT authority they do not hold, nor to
// hand it to the whole community by parking a role at position 0.
func TestAStafferCannotMintARoleCarryingBitsTheyDoNotHold(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	api.NewRoles(e.Repo, e.Clk, "dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	staff := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "staff", 10,
		api.PermViewChannel|api.PermSendMessages|api.PermManageRoles)
	member, memberTok := e.NewUser("staffer")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, memberTok)
	grant(t, e, cid, ownerTok, member, staff, http.StatusNoContent)

	// Below their own highest role, so the position bound passes — but the bits
	// are not theirs.
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/roles", memberTok,
		[]any{"sneaky", uint64(0), uint64(5), uint64(api.PermAdministrator), uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0)})
	if status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("minting an administrator role = %d, want 403 (%x)", status, body)
	}
	if code := e.ErrCode(body); code != "E_FORBIDDEN" {
		t.Fatalf("code = %s", code)
	}
	// The same request for bits the staffer does hold succeeds.
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/roles", memberTok,
		[]any{"fine", uint64(0), uint64(5), uint64(api.PermSendMessages), uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0)}); status != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("minting a role within their own bits = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}
	// And a PATCH cannot smuggle the bit in afterwards.
	low := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "low", 5, api.PermAddReactions)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/roles/"+low.String(), memberTok,
		[]any{nil, nil, nil, uint64(api.PermAdministrator), nil, nil, nil}); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("patching in an administrator bit = %d, want 403", status)
	}
}

func TestNoRoleCanBeMovedToPositionZero(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	api.NewRoles(e.Repo, e.Clk, "dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	role := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "staff", 10, api.PermViewChannel|api.PermManageRoles)

	// Position 0 is @everyone's and is implicitly held by every member, so a role
	// parked there would hand its bits to the whole community. Even the owner is
	// refused, because there is no legitimate reason to have two.
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/roles/"+role.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, nil, uint64(0), nil, nil, nil, nil})
	if status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("PATCH position=0 = %d, want 400 (%x)", status, body)
	}
	if code := e.ErrCode(body); code != "E_INVALID_REQUEST" {
		t.Fatalf("code = %s", code)
	}
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/roles", ownerTok,
		[]any{"also no", uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0), uint64(0)}); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("POST position=0 = %d, want 400", status)
	}
	// The @everyone role itself may still be edited — just not moved.
	roles, err := e.Repo.ListRoles(t.Context(), cid)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ListRoles: %v", err)
	}
	everyone := roles[0]
	if everyone.Position != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("roles[0] is not @everyone: %+v", everyone)
	}
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/roles/"+everyone.ID.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, nil, nil, uint64(api.PermViewChannel), nil, nil, nil}); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("the owner could not edit @everyone's permissions")
	}
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/roles/"+everyone.ID.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, nil, uint64(7), nil, nil, nil, nil}); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatal("@everyone was moved off position 0")
	}
}

func TestRemovingARoleThatOpenedAPrivateChannelSchedulesRemoves(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	api.NewRoles(e.Repo, e.Clk, "dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	ch, _, _ := newChannel(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 0, 0 /* e2ee */, 0 /* private */, "secret")
	role := createRole(t, e, cid, ownerTok, "insiders", 10, api.PermViewChannel|api.PermSendMessages)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPut,
		"/v1/channels/"+ch.String()+"/overwrites/0/"+role.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{uint64(api.PermViewChannel), uint64(0)}); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("PUT role overwrite failed")
	}
	member, memberTok := e.NewUser("insider")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, memberTok)
	grant(t, e, cid, ownerTok, member, role, http.StatusNoContent)

	members, err := e.Repo.ListChannelMembers(t.Context(), ch)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ListChannelMembers: %v", err)
	}
	if !slices.Contains(members, member) {
		t.Fatal("the grant did not materialise channel_members")
	}

	// Revoking the role drops the materialised eligibility, which is what task 7
	// turns into delivery-service Removes.
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodDelete,
		"/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/members/"+member.String()+"/roles/"+role.String(),
		ownerTok, nil); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("DELETE role grant failed")
	}
	members, _ = e.Repo.ListChannelMembers(t.Context(), ch)
	if slices.Contains(members, member) {
		t.Fatal("channel_members still lists a user whose role was revoked")
	}
}
```

`createRole`, `joinCommunity`, `grant` and `mustChannel` are four helpers at the bottom of the
file: `createRole` posts to `/v1/communities/{id}/roles` and returns the id; `joinCommunity` posts
to `/join`; `grant` PUTs `/members/{user}/roles/{role}` and asserts the status; `mustChannel` calls
`e.Repo.GetChannel`.

`channel_members` is created by task 7; until then the two `ListChannelMembers` assertions in
`TestRemovingARoleThatOpenedAPrivateChannelSchedulesRemoves` fail, so **that one test is written
here and enabled in task 7** — it carries `t.Skip("channel_members lands in task 7")` as its first
line in this task's commit and task 7 removes the line. The first two tests pass here.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the role and overwrite routes**

Extend `internal/api/roles.go` — task 1 created it with `moderatorBits`, `requireSecondFactor`, the
`Roles` type and the grant route. This task **adds** the `res` field and **replaces** the `Register`
body with the full seven-route table; the constructor keeps its task-1 signature so no call site
changes:

```go
type Roles struct {
	repo store.Repository
	clk  clock.Clock
	rpID string
	log  *slog.Logger
	res  *Resolver // added by task 3
}

func NewRoles(repo store.Repository, clk clock.Clock, rpID string, log *slog.Logger) *Roles {
	return &Roles{repo: repo, clk: clk, rpID: rpID, log: log, res: NewResolver(repo)}
}

func (h *Roles) Register(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /v1/communities/{id}/roles", h.create)
	mux.HandleFunc("PATCH /v1/roles/{id}", h.patch)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/roles/{id}", h.delete)
	mux.HandleFunc("PUT /v1/communities/{id}/members/{user_id}/roles/{role_id}", h.grant)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/communities/{id}/members/{user_id}/roles/{role_id}", h.revoke)
	mux.HandleFunc("PUT /v1/channels/{id}/overwrites/{kind}/{target_id}", h.putOverwrite)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/channels/{id}/overwrites/{kind}/{target_id}", h.deleteOverwrite)
}

// grant adds one role to one member. Three rules, in order: the granter holds
// PermManageRoles community-wide; the role's position is strictly below the
// granter's highest held role (the owner is above everything); and, when the
// community sets require_mod_2fa and the role carries a moderation bit, the
// *target* holds a second factor.
func (h *Roles) grant(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, ok := auth.SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		server.WriteError(w, errUnauthenticated())
		return
	}
	cid, err := server.PathID(r, "id")
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	target, err := server.PathID(r, "user_id")
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	roleID, err := server.PathID(r, "role_id")
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	com, err := h.repo.GetCommunity(r.Context(), cid)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, notFound(err))
		return
	}
	snap, err := LoadSnapshot(r.Context(), h.repo, cid, s.UserID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, notFound(err))
		return
	}
	if !snap.Resolve(s.UserID).Has(PermManageRoles) {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden, "manage roles"))
		return
	}
	role, err := h.repo.GetRole(r.Context(), roleID)
	// A store fault is not a 404: only ErrNotFound and a role belonging to another
	// community are. Collapsing both into 404 would hide a disk error behind an
	// authorisation answer.
	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if err != nil || role.CommunityID != cid {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeNotFound, "no such role"))
		return
	}
	if top, _ := snap.Highest(s.UserID); s.UserID != com.Owner && role.Position >= top {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"a role at or above your highest role cannot be granted"))
		return
	}
	if com.RequireMod2FA == 1 && Bits(role.Allow)&moderatorBits != 0 {
		if err := requireSecondFactor(r.Context(), h.repo, target, h.rpID); err != nil {
			server.WriteError(w, err)
			return
		}
	}
	if _, err := h.repo.GetMember(r.Context(), cid, target); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeNotFound, "not a member"))
		return
	}
	now := h.clk.Now().Unix()
	if err := h.repo.Tx(r.Context(), func(tx store.Repository) error {
		if err := tx.PutMemberRole(r.Context(), cid, target, roleID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return tx.Audit(r.Context(), store.AuditRow{
			Actor: &s.UserID, Action: "role.grant", Target: target.String(),
			Detail: roleID.String(), At: now,
		})
	}); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	// The eligibility materialiser runs AFTER the transaction commits, never
	// inside it: it calls the delivery service, which writes through the same
	// single-writer pool (§4.7 SetMaxOpenConns(1)) and would deadlock against the
	// open transaction. Task 7 gives it its body; a failure here is logged and
	// re-driven, because channel_members is derived state.
	if err := syncChannelEligibility(r.Context(), h.repo, cid, target, now); err != nil {
		h.log.Error("sync channel eligibility", "community", cid, "user", target, "err", err)
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

`revoke` is the mirror image with `DeleteMemberRole` and `action: "role.revoke"`, and the same
post-commit `syncChannelEligibility` call.

**`create` and `patch` carry four checks, not three**, and the last two are what keep
`PermManageRoles` from being a path to `PermAll`:

1. the actor holds `PermManageRoles` community-wide;
2. the role's position is strictly below the actor's highest held role (the owner is above
   everything);
3. **`position == 0` is refused on both verbs**, with `400 E_INVALID_REQUEST`. Position 0 is
   `@everyone`'s, `Resolve` grants it to every member by identity, and the community's own
   `@everyone` is the only role ever created there. `patch` refuses a request that sets
   `position = 0` **and** a request that would move the role currently at position 0 anywhere else,
   so `Snapshot.Everyone` is unambiguous for the life of the community;
4. **the requested `allow` and `deny` are masked by the actor's own resolved bits.** A holder of
   `PermManageRoles` at position 10 could otherwise create a role at position 5 carrying
   `PermAdministrator`, grant it to themselves — the position bound passes, 5 < 10 — and resolve to
   `PermAll`. The owner and any holder of `PermAdministrator` resolve to `PermAll`, so the mask is a
   no-op for them:

```go
// grantable is the set of bits actor may put in a role's allow or deny. Nobody
// creates authority they do not hold.
func (h *Roles) grantable(snap Snapshot, actor id.ID) Bits { return snap.Resolve(actor) }

// checkBits refuses a create or patch that would name a bit the actor does not
// hold. allow and deny are checked the same way: the power to DENY a permission
// community-wide is as much authority as the power to grant it.
func checkBits(have, allow, deny Bits) error {
	if bad := (allow | deny) &^ have; bad != 0 {
		return server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"a role may not carry a permission you do not hold (%#x)", uint64(bad))
	}
	return nil
}
```

`delete` keeps checks 1 and 2 plus "you may not delete a role at or above your own", and refuses to
delete the role at position 0 at all. `putOverwrite` and `deleteOverwrite` resolve `{kind}` as `0`
or `1` (anything else is `E_INVALID_REQUEST`), require `PermManageRoles` **in that channel**, mask
`allow`/`deny` with `PermAll` **and** with the actor's `ResolveChannel` bits by the same
`checkBits`, refuse a pair that sets the same bit in both, and call `MaterialiseChannelMembers`
(task 7) for that channel after the transaction commits.

> **Why `@everyone` is identified by position at rest and by id at run time.** Adding a
> `communities.role_everyone` column would be the other way to key it, and was rejected: it
> duplicates a fact the `roles` table already holds, and a column can drift from the row it names.
> Instead the *invariant* is enforced at the only two places that can break it — `create` and
> `patch` both refuse position 0 — and `LoadSnapshot` reads the id once, so nothing downstream ever
> compares a position again.

`syncChannelEligibility` is declared here and implemented in task 7, where `channel_members`
exists:

```go

// private channel of the community, from the resolver's verdict. Task 7 gives it
// its body and its delivery-service side; here it is a no-op so that the call
// sites are already in place.
//
//nolint:unparam,revive // every parameter is used once task 7 fills the body.
func syncChannelEligibility(ctx context.Context, repo store.Repository, communityID, userID id.ID, now int64) error {
	return nil
}
```

In `internal/api/channels.go`, `manageable` becomes:

```go
func (c *Channels) manageable(r *http.Request) (store.ChannelRow, auth.Session, error) {
	row, s, err := c.visible(r)
	if err != nil {
		return row, s, err
	}
	if row.CommunityID == nil {
		return row, s, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden, "a DM has no manager")
	}
	if err := c.res.Require(r.Context(), s.UserID, row, PermManageChannels); err != nil {
		return row, s, err
	}
	return row, s, nil
}
```

with `res *Resolver` added to `Channels` and set in `NewChannels`; `visible` gains the same
treatment against `PermViewChannel`. In `create`, the owner check becomes a `Snapshot` load plus
`snap.Resolve(s.UserID).Has(PermManageChannels)` — there is no channel yet, so there are no
overwrites to apply. In `internal/api/communities.go`, `ownerOnly` keeps the owner check for
`DELETE /v1/communities/{id}` and `PATCH` additionally accepts `PermManageCommunity`.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"slices"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/api"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/store"
)

// recordingDS records the proposals a handler sends to the delivery service.
// The delivery service's own behaviour is tested in internal/ds; here the
// subject is the membership bookkeeping that drives it.
type recordingDS struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	Removes []struct {
		Group id.ID
		Leaf  uint32
	}
	Adds   []struct{ Group, Device id.ID }
	Closed []id.ID
}

func (d *recordingDS) ProposeAdd(_ context.Context, g, dev, _ id.ID) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.Adds = append(d.Adds, struct{ Group, Device id.ID }{g, dev})
	return nil
}

func (d *recordingDS) ProposeRemove(_ context.Context, g id.ID, leaf uint32, _ id.ID) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.Removes = append(d.Removes, struct {
		Group id.ID
		Leaf  uint32
	}{g, leaf})
	return nil
}

func (d *recordingDS) ProposeAddBatch(ctx context.Context, g id.ID, devices []id.ID) error {
	for _, dev := range devices {
		if err := d.ProposeAdd(ctx, g, dev, id.New()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

func (d *recordingDS) Close(_ context.Context, g id.ID) error {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.Closed = append(d.Closed, g)
	return nil
}

func TestBanRemovesMembershipAndIssuesDSRemoves(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	dsvc := &recordingDS{}
	api.NewBans(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)

	ch, _, _ := newChannel(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 0, 0, 0, "secret")
	target, targetTok := e.NewUser("troll")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, targetTok)

	// An MLS text group bound to that channel, with the target at leaf 3.
	group := seedTextGroup(t, e, ch, cid)
	seedMember(t, e, group, target, 3)

	path := "/v1/communities/" + cid.String() + "/bans/" + target.String()
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPut, path, ownerTok,
		[]any{"spam", nil}); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatalf("PUT ban = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}

	if _, err := e.Repo.GetMember(t.Context(), cid, target); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("the ban left the membership row: %v", err)
	}
	if len(dsvc.Removes) != 1 || dsvc.Removes[0].Group != group || dsvc.Removes[0].Leaf != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("Removes = %+v, want one for leaf 3 of %x", dsvc.Removes, group)
	}
	// Re-joining is refused while the ban stands.
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", targetTok, []any{nil}); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("join while banned = %d", status)
	} else if code := e.ErrCode(body); code != "E_FORBIDDEN" {
		t.Fatalf("code = %s", code)
	}
}

func TestABanWithAnExpiryLapses(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	dsvc := &recordingDS{}
	api.NewBans(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	target, targetTok := e.NewUser("troll")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, targetTok)

	until := uint64(e.Clk.Now().Add(time.Hour).Unix())
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPut,
		"/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/bans/"+target.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{"cool off", until}); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("PUT ban failed")
	}
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", targetTok, []any{nil}); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatal("join during the ban was allowed")
	}
	e.Clk.Advance(2 * time.Hour)
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", targetTok, []any{nil}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("join after the ban lapsed = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}
	// The row survives the lapse — GET /bans still shows it, so a moderator can
	// see the history — but it no longer gates.
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodGet, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/bans", ownerTok, nil)
	if status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("GET bans = %d", status)
	}
	var bans [][]cbor.RawMessage
	mustUnmarshalBody(t, body, &bans)
	if len(bans) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("bans = %d rows, want 1", len(bans))
	}
}

func TestAKickRemovesMembershipButWritesNoBanRow(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	dsvc := &recordingDS{}
	api.NewBans(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	target, targetTok := e.NewUser("noisy")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, targetTok)

	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodDelete,
		"/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/members/"+target.String(), ownerTok, nil); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("kick failed")
	}
	if _, err := e.Repo.GetMember(t.Context(), cid, target); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatal("the kick left the membership row")
	}
	if _, err := e.Repo.GetBan(t.Context(), cid, target); !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
		t.Fatal("a kick wrote a ban row")
	}
	// A kicked user may rejoin immediately.
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", targetTok, []any{nil}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal("a kicked user could not rejoin")
	}
}

func TestEveryModerationActionWritesAnAuditRow(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	owner := ownerOf(t, e, cid)
	dsvc := &recordingDS{}
	api.NewBans(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	target, targetTok := e.NewUser("troll")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, targetTok)

	base := "/v1/communities/" + cid.String()
	steps := []struct {
		method, path string
		body         any
		action       string
	}{
		{http.MethodPut, base + "/bans/" + target.String(), []any{"spam", nil}, "ban.create"},
		{http.MethodDelete, base + "/bans/" + target.String(), nil, "ban.delete"},
	}
	for _, s := range steps {
		if status, _ := e.Do(s.method, s.path, ownerTok, s.body); status != http.StatusNoContent {
			t.Fatalf("%s %s = %d", s.method, s.path, status)
		}
	}
	rows, err := e.Repo.ListAudit(t.Context(), 0, 100)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ListAudit: %v", err)
	}
	var seen []string
	for _, row := range rows {
		if row.Actor == nil || *row.Actor != owner {
			t.Fatalf("audit row %+v does not name the actor", row)
		}
		seen = append(seen, row.Action)
	}
	for _, want := range []string{"ban.create", "ban.delete"} {
		if !slices.Contains(seen, want) {
			t.Fatalf("no audit row for %s: %v", want, seen)
		}
	}
}
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE bans (
  community_id BLOB    NOT NULL CHECK (length(community_id) = 16)
                       REFERENCES communities(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id      BLOB    NOT NULL CHECK (length(user_id) = 16)
                       REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  reason       TEXT    NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  by_user      BLOB    NOT NULL CHECK (length(by_user) = 16),

  expires      INTEGER,

) STRICT;
```

Append to both `structure.sql` query files:

```sql
-- name: PutBan :exec
INSERT INTO bans (community_id, user_id, reason, by_user, created, expires)
VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
ON CONFLICT (community_id, user_id) DO UPDATE SET
  reason = excluded.reason, by_user = excluded.by_user,
  created = excluded.created, expires = excluded.expires;

-- name: GetBan :one
SELECT community_id, user_id, reason, by_user, created, expires
FROM bans WHERE community_id = ? AND user_id = ?;

-- name: ListBans :many
SELECT community_id, user_id, reason, by_user, created, expires
FROM bans WHERE community_id = ? ORDER BY created DESC, user_id;

-- name: DeleteBan :execrows
DELETE FROM bans WHERE community_id = ? AND user_id = ?;
```

```sql
       epoch, seq, group_info_blob, tree_hash, public_group_state, external_sender_key_id,
       e2ee_version, media_version, policy_version, epoch_unknown, heal_deadline,
       created, closed_at
FROM mls_groups
WHERE target_id = ? AND kind = ? AND closed_at IS NULL
ORDER BY created;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the membership bridge**

Create `internal/api/membership.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/store"
)

// DS is the delivery-service surface the api package calls. It is a subset of
// *ds.DS (interfaces.md §6.2) so that the api tests can record the calls a
// membership change makes without standing in for the delivery service itself.
type DS interface {
	ProposeAdd(ctx context.Context, groupID, deviceID, actionID id.ID) error
	ProposeRemove(ctx context.Context, groupID id.ID, leaf uint32, actionID id.ID) error
	ProposeAddBatch(ctx context.Context, groupID id.ID, devices []id.ID) error
	Close(ctx context.Context, groupID id.ID) error
}

// GroupKind values, protocol/01 "Group kinds".
const (
	GroupText        uint8 = 0
	GroupCall        uint8 = 1
	GroupPairing     uint8 = 2
	GroupInteraction uint8 = 3
)

// RemoveUserFromChannelGroups issues one delivery-service Remove per live leaf
// of userID in the text and call groups bound to channelID. Every Remove of one
// membership change shares an action_id, so that the delivery service's
// re-issue path (interfaces.md §4.3, mls_pending_proposals.action_id) keeps
// them together across epochs.
func RemoveUserFromChannelGroups(ctx context.Context, repo store.Repository, dsvc DS, channelID, userID id.ID) error {
	actionID := id.New()
	for _, kind := range []uint8{GroupText, GroupCall} {
		groups, err := repo.GroupsForTarget(ctx, channelID, kind)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, g := range groups {
			members, err := repo.ListMembers(ctx, g.GroupID)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			for _, m := range members {
				if m.UserID != userID || m.RemovedEpoch != nil {
					continue
				}
				if err := dsvc.ProposeRemove(ctx, g.GroupID, m.LeafIndex, actionID); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// RemoveUserFromCommunityGroups does the same across every live channel of a
// community. It is the kick and ban path.
//
// It MUST NOT be called from inside a store.Repository.Tx. internal/ds writes
// through its own store.Repository, which is the same *sql.DB write pool, and
// §4.7 sets that pool to SetMaxOpenConns(1): the enclosing transaction holds the
// single write connection, so the delivery service's first write blocks forever.
// busy_timeout cannot break it, because the contention is in Go's sql.DB pool and
// not in SQLite's locking. Every caller commits its rows first and calls this
// afterwards; task 7's MaterialiseChannelMembers is split the same way and for
// the same reason.
func RemoveUserFromCommunityGroups(ctx context.Context, repo store.Repository, dsvc DS, communityID, userID id.ID) error {
	channels, err := repo.ListChannels(ctx, communityID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, ch := range channels {
		if err := repo.DeleteChannelMember(ctx, ch.ID, userID); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			return err
		}
		if err := RemoveUserFromChannelGroups(ctx, repo, dsvc, ch.ID, userID); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// BanStands reports whether a ban row still gates: a row with no expiry always
// does, a row with an expiry only until that second.
func BanStands(b store.BanRow, now int64) bool {
	return b.Expires == nil || *b.Expires > now
}
```

`ListMembers` returns `store.MemberRow` with `LeafIndex uint32` and `RemovedEpoch *uint64`, from
§4.3's `mls_members`. `DeleteChannelMember` exists in §4.1; task 7 creates its table, so until then
it returns `store.ErrNotFound` from the stub and the `errors.Is` guard above absorbs it.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the ban routes and rewire the kick**

Create `internal/api/bans.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/auth"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/clock"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/server"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/store"
)

type Bans struct {
	repo store.Repository
	dsvc DS
	clk  clock.Clock
	log  *slog.Logger
}

func NewBans(repo store.Repository, dsvc DS, clk clock.Clock, log *slog.Logger) *Bans {
	return &Bans{repo: repo, dsvc: dsvc, clk: clk, log: log}
}

func (b *Bans) Register(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("PUT /v1/communities/{id}/bans/{user_id}", b.put)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/communities/{id}/bans/{user_id}", b.delete)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /v1/communities/{id}/bans", b.list)
}

type putBanReq struct {
	_       struct{} `cbor:",toarray"`
	Reason  string
	Expires *uint64
}

const maxBanReasonBytes = 512

func (b *Bans) put(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, cid, target, snap, err := b.moderate(r, PermBanMembers)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	var req putBanReq
	if err := server.DecodeBody(w, r, 64*1024, &req); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if len(req.Reason) > maxBanReasonBytes {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"reason must be at most %d bytes", maxBanReasonBytes))
		return
	}
	now := b.clk.Now().Unix()
	if req.Expires != nil && int64(*req.Expires) <= now {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"expires must be in the future"))
		return
	}
	// A moderator may not ban someone at or above their own highest role, and
	// nobody may ban the owner.
	if err := b.outranks(r.Context(), snap, s.UserID, target, cid); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	var expires *int64
	if req.Expires != nil {
		v := int64(*req.Expires)
		expires = &v
	}
	// The rows first, in one transaction …
	if err := b.repo.Tx(r.Context(), func(tx store.Repository) error {
		if err := tx.PutBan(r.Context(), store.BanRow{
			CommunityID: cid, UserID: target, Reason: req.Reason,
			ByUser: s.UserID, Created: now, Expires: expires,
		}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := tx.DeleteMember(r.Context(), cid, target); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			return err
		}
		return tx.Audit(r.Context(), store.AuditRow{
			Actor: &s.UserID, Action: "ban.create", Target: target.String(),
			Detail: req.Reason, At: now,
		})
	}); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	// … and the delivery service AFTER the commit. See the comment on
	// RemoveUserFromCommunityGroups: the DS writes through the same
	// single-connection write pool, so calling it inside the transaction above
	// is a self-deadlock that no timeout can break.
	//
	// If the process dies between the commit and this call the ban stands and the
	// Removes are outstanding: the eligibility materialiser (task 7) re-drives
	// them on the next membership change of that community, and the DS's own
	// re-issue path keeps a partially committed Remove alive across epochs.
	if err := RemoveUserFromCommunityGroups(r.Context(), b.repo, b.dsvc, cid, target); err != nil {
		b.log.Error("remove banned user from groups", "community", cid, "user", target, "err", err)
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}
```

`TestBanRemovesMembershipAndIssuesDSRemoves` drives the recording double, which touches no database.
Add one more test that drives the same path against a **real single-connection SQLite pool** and a
`ds.DS` built over the same repository, so the deadlock this split avoids is actually exercised:

```go
func TestBanAgainstARealPoolDoesNotDeadlock(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := channelEnv(t)
	// e.Repo is sqlite.New(write, read, clk) with the §4.7 write pool, i.e.
	// SetMaxOpenConns(1). realDS writes through the same repository.
	dsvc := newRealDS(t, e.Repo)
	api.NewBans(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	ch, _, _ := newChannel(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 0, 0, 0, "secret")
	target, targetTok := e.NewUser("troll")
	joinCommunity(t, e, cid, targetTok)
	group := seedTextGroup(t, e, ch, cid)
	seedMember(t, e, group, target, 3)

	done := make(chan int, 1)
	go func() {
		status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPut,
			"/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/bans/"+target.String(), ownerTok, []any{"spam", nil})
		done <- status
	}()
	select {
	case status := <-done:
		if status != http.StatusNoContent {
			t.Fatalf("PUT ban = %d", status)
		}
	case <-time.After(10 * time.Second):
		t.Fatal("the ban path deadlocked: a delivery-service write is inside the ban transaction")
	}
}
```

`newRealDS` builds the real `*ds.DS` over `e.Repo` with the test clock, which is what makes the
single-writer contention real rather than mocked.

`delete` mirrors it with `DeleteBan` and `action: "ban.delete"`, and issues no proposals — an
unbanned user is not re-added, they re-join. `list` requires `PermBanMembers` and encodes the
`[[user_id, reason, by_user, created, expires|null]]` array of the header table. `moderate` is:

```go
// moderate resolves {id} and {user_id}, requires the session to hold want
// community-wide, and returns the caller's snapshot.
func (b *Bans) moderate(r *http.Request, want Bits) (auth.Session, id.ID, id.ID, Snapshot, error) {
	var zero Snapshot
	s, ok := auth.SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		return s, id.ID{}, id.ID{}, zero, errUnauthenticated()
	}
	cid, err := server.PathID(r, "id")
	if err != nil {
		return s, id.ID{}, id.ID{}, zero, err
	}
	var target id.ID
	if r.PathValue("user_id") != "" {
		if target, err = server.PathID(r, "user_id"); err != nil {
			return s, cid, target, zero, err
		}
	}
	snap, err := LoadSnapshot(r.Context(), b.repo, cid, s.UserID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return s, cid, target, zero, notFound(err)
	}
	if !snap.Resolve(s.UserID).Has(want) {
		return s, cid, target, zero, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden, "missing permission")
	}
	return s, cid, target, snap, nil
}

// outranks refuses a moderation action against the owner or against a member
// whose highest role is at or above the actor's.
//
// It loads a SECOND snapshot for the target, because Snapshot.Held is one user's
// grants and Highest reads it. Snapshot.Highest is the only accessor; there is no
// HighestFor. Communities.removeMember (the kick verb) calls this same function
// with this same signature.
func (b *Bans) outranks(ctx context.Context, snap Snapshot, actor, target, cid id.ID) error {
	if target == snap.Owner {
		return server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden, "the owner cannot be moderated")
	}
	if actor == snap.Owner {
		return nil
	}
	targetSnap, err := LoadSnapshot(ctx, b.repo, cid, target, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	actorTop, _ := snap.Highest(actor)
	targetTop, _ := targetSnap.Highest(target)
	if targetTop >= actorTop {
		return server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"the target holds a role at or above yours")
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Close a deleted community's and channel's groups**

In `internal/api/communities.go`'s `delete` handler. The collection is inside the transaction, the
delivery-service calls are **after** it — same rule as the ban path, same reason: `ds.Close` writes
through the single-connection write pool the open transaction is holding.

```go
	// Inside the transaction: read what will be closed, then tombstone.
	// ListChannels filters deleted_at IS NULL, so it must run BEFORE
	// DeleteChannelsOfCommunity or it returns nothing.
	var toClose []id.ID
	if err := c.repo.Tx(r.Context(), func(tx store.Repository) error {
		channels, err := tx.ListChannels(r.Context(), row.ID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, ch := range channels {
			for _, kind := range []uint8{GroupText, GroupCall} {
				groups, err := tx.GroupsForTarget(r.Context(), ch.ID, kind)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				for _, g := range groups {
					toClose = append(toClose, g.GroupID)
				}
			}
		}
		if _, err := tx.DeleteChannelsOfCommunity(r.Context(), row.ID, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := tx.SoftDeleteCommunity(r.Context(), row.ID, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return tx.Audit(r.Context(), store.AuditRow{
			Actor: &s.UserID, Action: "community.delete", Target: row.ID.String(),
			Detail: "", At: now,
		})
	}); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, notFound(err))
		return
	}
	// After the commit. A crash between the two leaves the community tombstoned
	// and the groups open; they carry no live channel, the retention sweeper
	// closes them, and dillad doctor's blob/group consistency leg reports them.
	for _, g := range toClose {
		if err := c.dsvc.Close(r.Context(), g); err != nil {
			c.log.Error("close group of deleted community", "group", g, "err", err)
		}
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
```

`internal/api/channels.go`'s `delete` is split the same way for the one channel being removed: the
transaction tombstones the row and writes the audit entry, and the `ds.Close` calls follow it.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the scenario**

Create `testkit/scenarios/kick_while_offline_then_join.dsl`:

```
# Invariant 5 and 6: a kick issued while the target is offline freezes the group
# until the instance's Remove is committed, and the target's later join attempt
# is refused by the community gate, not by MLS.
#
# The bare `ds` directive selects the HTTP delivery service and takes its address
# from the harness's --ds flag (§7.1/§7.3 start dillad on a random loopback port).
# A literal address here would override an address the file cannot know.
ds
instance i1
client alice
client bob
client carol
group g1 alice
join g1 bob
join g1 carol
sync
send alice g1 "before"
expect_decrypts bob g1 "before"
go_offline bob
kick alice bob
# The instance's Remove is outstanding, so an external commit is refused while
# any member device is online.
expect_425 carol g1
# Alice commits it; bob's leaf is gone.
sync
expect_frame mls.epoch_changed group=g1
go_online bob
expect_reject bob g1 status=403 code=E_LEAF_NOT_CURRENT
```

`kick <client> <target>` is the verb interfaces.md §7.2 adds in Plan 1b task 28; `expect_425` and
`expect_frame` come from the same list. **The `ds` directive takes no argument in any scenario this
plan writes**: §7.2 makes the directive's *presence* select `HttpDs` and §7.3 has the Go harness
pass `--ds http://127.0.0.1:<port>` after starting dillad on a random port, so the file must not
name an address. Plan 1b task 28 states that precedence — flag over directive, and a directive with
an argument is a parse error — in `protocol/02`'s testkit note. The scenario runs under the Go harness
(`internal/testkit`), which Plan 1b task 29 builds; this task adds only the file and the
`internal/ds` Go test that asserts the same three states, so the scenario is executable the moment
the harness lands.

```go
package api_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"sync"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/fxamacker/cbor/v2"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/api"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/auth"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/server"
)

func inviteEnv(t *testing.T) (*env, id.ID, string) {
	t.Helper()
	e, cid, tok := channelEnv(t)
	h := api.NewInvites(e.Repo, e.Clk, "https://dilla.example", slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler))
	// Register puts the three community routes on the mux; RegisterPublic puts
	// GET /i/{code} and POST /v1/invites/redeem there. Plan 1a's serve wires the
	// second set, so the harness must too — newEnv registers nothing by itself,
	// and without this the preview tests below get 404, not 200.
	h.Register(e.Mux)
	h.RegisterPublic(e.Mux)
	// The preview bucket is middleware, not handler code, so it has to be wrapped
	// around the mux the same way serve wraps it.
	e.UseRateLimiter(server.NewRateLimiter(inviteRateConfig(), e.Clk))
	return e, cid, tok
}

// mintInvite creates one community invite and returns its plaintext code.
func mintInvite(t *testing.T, e *env, cid id.ID, tok string, maxUses, ttl uint64) string {
	t.Helper()
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/invites", tok,
		[]any{maxUses, ttl, uint64(0)})
	if status != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("POST invite = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}
	var out []cbor.RawMessage
	mustUnmarshalBody(t, body, &out)
	var code string
	mustUnmarshal(t, out[1], &code)
	return code
}

func TestACommunityInviteAuthorisesAJoin(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	// Make the community closed: no invite, no join.
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, []byte(`{"join":"invite"}`), nil, nil}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal("PATCH policy failed")
	}
	_, memberTok := e.NewUser("newcomer")
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", memberTok, []any{nil}); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("join without an invite = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 1, 3600)
	if status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", memberTok, []any{code}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("join with an invite = %d (%x)", status, body)
	}
	if _, err := e.Repo.GetMember(t.Context(), cid, userOf(t, e, memberTok)); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the join did not write a member row: %v", err)
	}
}

func TestThePreviewNeverMutatesAndCarriesNoStore(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 5, 3600)

	resp, err := e.Srv.Client().Get(e.Srv.URL + "/i/" + code)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GET /i/{code}: %v", err)
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("GET /i/{code} = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if got := resp.Header.Get("Cache-Control"); got != "no-store" {
		t.Fatalf("Cache-Control = %q, want no-store", got)
	}
	if got := resp.Header.Get("Referrer-Policy"); got != "no-referrer" {
		t.Fatalf("Referrer-Policy = %q", got)
	}
	inv, err := e.Repo.GetInviteByHash(t.Context(), auth.InviteHash(code))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInviteByHash: %v", err)
	}
	if inv.UsedCount != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the preview consumed a use: used_count = %d", inv.UsedCount)
	}
}

func TestMaxUsesIsEnforcedUnderConcurrency(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 3, 3600)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, []byte(`{"join":"invite"}`), nil, nil}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal("PATCH policy failed")
	}

	const attempts = 12
	toks := make([]string, attempts)
	for i := range toks {
		_, toks[i] = e.NewUser(fmt.Sprintf("u%02d", i))
	}
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	results := make([]int, attempts)
	for i := range attempts {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			results[i], _ = e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", toks[i], []any{code})
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	var ok int
	for _, s := range results {
		switch s {
		case http.StatusOK:
			ok++
		case http.StatusGone, http.StatusTooManyRequests:
		default:
			t.Fatalf("unexpected status %d", s)
		}
	}
	if ok != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("%d joins succeeded, want exactly 3", ok)
	}
	inv, err := e.Repo.GetInviteByHash(t.Context(), auth.InviteHash(code))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInviteByHash: %v", err)
	}
	if inv.UsedCount != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("used_count = %d, want 3", inv.UsedCount)
	}
}

func TestARevokedInviteIsGone(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 5, 3600)
	inv, err := e.Repo.GetInviteByHash(t.Context(), auth.InviteHash(code))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInviteByHash: %v", err)
	}
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodDelete, "/v1/invites/"+inv.ID.String(), ownerTok, nil); status != http.StatusNoContent {
		t.Fatal("DELETE invite failed")
	}
	_, tok := e.NewUser("late")
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", tok, []any{code})
	if status != http.StatusGone {
		t.Fatalf("join with a revoked invite = %d", status)
	}
	if got := e.ErrCode(body); got != "E_INVITE_INVALID" {
		t.Fatalf("code = %s, want E_INVITE_INVALID", got)
	}

	// Expiry is the same refusal.
	code2 := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 5, 60)
	e.Clk.Advance(2 * time.Minute)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", tok, []any{code2}); status != http.StatusGone {
		t.Fatalf("join with an expired invite = %d", status)
	}
}

// The burst half only. Plan 1a's server.RateLimiter takes a clock.Clock but uses
// it solely to age buckets out; the token bucket itself is
// golang.org/x/time/rate, whose Allow() reads time.Now(). Advancing e.Clk
// therefore refills nothing, and an assertion about refill would be a test of the
// wall clock. The refill rate is tested where it can be: in
// internal/server/ratelimit_test.go, against a limiter constructed with a
// known rate and driven by real elapsed time.
//
// Eight requests inside one burst window take well under a second, so "5 allowed,
// 3 limited" is deterministic.
func TestTheInviteBucketBurstsAtFive(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 1000, 3600)
	var ok, limited int
	for range 8 {
		switch status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodGet, "/i/"+code, "", nil); status {
		case http.StatusOK:
			ok++
		case http.StatusTooManyRequests:
			limited++
		default:
			t.Fatalf("unexpected status %d", status)
		}
	}
	if ok != 5 || limited != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("%d allowed and %d limited of 8, want 5 and 3 (burst 5)", ok, limited)
	}
	// A limited response carries Retry-After, which is what tells a client the
	// refill rate without the test having to wait for it.
	resp := e.Request(t, http.MethodGet, "/i/"+code, "", nil, nil)
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusTooManyRequests {
		t.Fatalf("status = %d", resp.StatusCode)
	}
	if resp.Header.Get("Retry-After") == "" {
		t.Fatal("a 429 carries no Retry-After")
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Extend `internal/auth/invite.go`**

`InviteHash` and `MatchInviteCode` live in **`internal/auth`**, beside `NewInviteCode` and

Add the canonicalisation round trip Plan 1a task 8 left at the code level, and the hash helper:

```go
// InviteHash is the only form of a code that is ever stored: SHA-256 of the
// canonical spelling. interfaces.md §4.3, invites.code_hash H32.
func InviteHash(code string) []byte {
	sum := sha256.Sum256([]byte(code))
	return sum[:]
}

// MatchInviteCode returns the hashes to try for a user-typed code: the
// canonical form plus every ambiguity resolution CanonicalizeInviteCode
// produces (1 may be I or L). At most four candidates for a 26-character code
// carrying one '1'.
func MatchInviteCode(typed string) ([][]byte, error) {
	forms, err := CanonicalizeInviteCode(typed)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([][]byte, 0, len(forms))
	for _, f := range forms {
		out = append(out, InviteHash(f))
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the routes**

Create the three handlers in `internal/api/invites.go` (Plan 1a task 8 created the file with the
instance-level `POST /v1/invites/redeem` and `GET /i/{code}`; this task adds the community scope):

```go
// Register puts the three community-scoped invite routes on the mux.
func (h *Invites) Register(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /v1/communities/{id}/invites", h.create)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /v1/communities/{id}/invites", h.list)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/invites/{id}", h.revoke)
}

// RegisterPublic puts the two unauthenticated routes on the mux. Plan 1a task 8
// wrote these handlers; the registration is split out so a test harness can ask
// for them without also getting the community routes, and so serve can put them
// behind the ("invite", clientIP) bucket on their own.
func (h *Invites) RegisterPublic(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /i/{code}", h.preview)
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /v1/invites/redeem", h.redeem)
}

const (
	maxInviteUses = 1000  // interfaces.md §4.3: max_uses BETWEEN 1 AND 1000
	maxInviteTTL  = 30 * 24 * 3600
)

type createInviteReq struct {
	_           struct{} `cbor:",toarray"`
	MaxUses     uint64
	TTLSeconds  uint64
	GrantsAdmin uint64
}

type createInviteResp struct {
	_        struct{} `cbor:",toarray"`
	InviteID id.ID
	Code     string
	URL      string
	Expires  int64
}

func (h *Invites) create(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, ok := auth.SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		server.WriteError(w, errUnauthenticated())
		return
	}
	cid, err := server.PathID(r, "id")
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	snap, err := LoadSnapshot(r.Context(), h.repo, cid, s.UserID, nil)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, notFound(err))
		return
	}
	bits := snap.Resolve(s.UserID)
	if !bits.Has(PermCreateInvite) {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden, "create invite"))
		return
	}
	var req createInviteReq
	if err := server.DecodeBody(w, r, 64*1024, &req); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if req.MaxUses < 1 || req.MaxUses > maxInviteUses {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"max_uses must be 1..%d", maxInviteUses))
		return
	}
	if req.TTLSeconds < 1 || req.TTLSeconds > maxInviteTTL {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"ttl_seconds must be 1..%d", maxInviteTTL))
		return
	}
	// grants_admin is an instance-level power; only an instance admin may set it
	// on a community invite, and it is refused outright to a mere community owner.
	if req.GrantsAdmin == 1 && !h.instanceAdmin(r.Context(), s.UserID) {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"grants_admin is an instance-admin power"))
		return
	}
	plain, hash := auth.NewInviteCode()
	now := h.clk.Now().Unix()
	row := store.InviteRow{
		ID: id.New(), CodeHash: hash, CommunityID: &cid, CreatedBy: &s.UserID,
		GrantsAdmin: uint8(req.GrantsAdmin), MaxUses: req.MaxUses, UsedCount: 0,
		Created: now, ExpiresAt: now + int64(req.TTLSeconds),
	}
	if err := h.repo.Tx(r.Context(), func(tx store.Repository) error {
		if err := tx.CreateInvite(r.Context(), row); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return tx.Audit(r.Context(), store.AuditRow{
			Actor: &s.UserID, Action: "invite.create", Target: row.ID.String(),
			// The code never reaches a log or an audit row; its 8-hex reference does.
			Detail: hex.EncodeToString(hash[:4]), At: now,
		})
	}); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if err := server.EncodeBody(w, http.StatusCreated, createInviteResp{
		InviteID: row.ID, Code: plain, URL: h.baseURL + "/i/" + plain, Expires: row.ExpiresAt,
	}); err != nil {
		h.log.Error("encode invite", "err", err)
	}
}
```

`list` requires `PermCreateInvite` and returns `ListInvites(ctx, &cid)`; `revoke` requires
`PermManageCommunity` (or that the caller created the invite) and calls `RevokeInvite`.

The preview handler Plan 1a registered gains the community leg: when `invites.community_id` is
non-null it reads the community name and renders it, still without mutating and still with
`Cache-Control: no-store` and `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer`. Its rate limiter is
`server.RateLimiter` keyed `("invite", clientIP)` with burst 5 and 0.1/s, which is
`limits.rate.invite_per_second = 0.1` and `invite_burst = 5` from §6.4; the same bucket meters
`POST /v1/communities/{id}/join`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Redeem the invite in `join`**

Replace the "task 5 replaces this" comment in `internal/api/communities.go`'s `join`:

```go
	policyRequiresInvite := gjsonJoinMode(row.PolicyJSON) == "invite"
	var invite *store.InviteRow
	if req.Invite != nil {
		hashes, err := auth.MatchInviteCode(*req.Invite)
		if err != nil {
			server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest, "malformed invite"))
			return
		}
		// Resolve WHICH candidate exists before trying to redeem it. §4.1 defines
		// RedeemInvite as one guarded UPDATE returning ErrExhausted whenever
		// RowsAffected != 1 — which is also what an unknown code_hash produces, so
		// a loop that treats ErrExhausted as terminal would answer 410 for the
		// first non-existent candidate of a legitimate code. CanonicalizeInviteCode
		// returns several candidates for any code containing a '1' (the I/L
		// ambiguity), so that is the common case, not the corner case.
		for _, h := range hashes {
			row, err := c.repo.GetInviteByHash(r.Context(), h)
			if errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
				continue
			}
			if err != nil {
				server.WriteError(w, err)
				return
			}
			redeemed, err := c.repo.RedeemInvite(r.Context(), row.CodeHash, c.clk.Now().Unix())
			switch {
			case err == nil:
				invite = &redeemed
			case errors.Is(err, store.ErrExhausted), errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound):
				// The row exists, so this is a real refusal: expired, exhausted or
				// revoked. It is not "try the next candidate".
				server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInviteInvalid,
					"the invite is expired, exhausted or revoked"))
				return
			default:
				server.WriteError(w, err)
				return
			}
			break
		}
		if invite == nil {
			server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInviteInvalid,
				"the invite is expired, exhausted or revoked"))
			return
		}
		if invite.CommunityID == nil || *invite.CommunityID != cid {
			server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInviteInvalid,
				"that invite is for another community"))
			return
		}
	} else if policyRequiresInvite {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"this community is invite only"))
		return
	}
```

`gjsonJoinMode` is a four-line `encoding/json` decode of `{"join": "..."}` into a struct with one
field — no JSON dependency is added. The redemption is `store.Invites.RedeemInvite`, whose §4.1
comment already fixes the concurrency rule: "one `UPDATE` guarded by `used_count < max_uses AND
revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now`; `ErrExhausted` unless `RowsAffected == 1`", which is what
makes `TestMaxUsesIsEnforcedUnderConcurrency` pass on a single-writer SQLite pool and on Postgres
alike.

**Order: `joinGate` first, then `RedeemInvite`.** The ban check and the account-age gate run
*before* redemption, so a refused join burns no use; put the `RedeemInvite` block **below** the
`joinGate` call. (Redeeming first and then refusing would consume a use on behalf of an account that
never joined, which is exactly how a one-use invite gets silently destroyed.)

Add a test for it beside the four above:

```go
func TestARefusedJoinBurnsNoInviteUse(t *testing.T) {
	e, cid, ownerTok := inviteEnv(t)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPatch, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String(), ownerTok,
		[]any{nil, nil, uint64(7 * 24 * 3600), nil}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal("PATCH min_account_age_seconds failed")
	}
	code := mintInvite(t, e, cid, ownerTok, 1, 3600)
	_, freshTok := e.NewUser("fresh") // created at e.Clk.Now(), so too young
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", freshTok,
		[]any{code}); status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatal("a too-young account was allowed to join")
	}
	inv, err := e.Repo.GetInviteByHash(t.Context(), auth.InviteHash(code))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInviteByHash: %v", err)
	}
	if inv.UsedCount != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("a refused join burned %d use(s)", inv.UsedCount)
	}
	// And the one use is still there for someone who passes the gate.
	e.Clk.Advance(8 * 24 * time.Hour)
	if status, _ := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/communities/"+cid.String()+"/join", freshTok,
		[]any{code}); status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatal("the invite did not survive the refused join")
	}
}
```

Add a second test that a code whose canonicalisation produces two candidates still redeems:
`TestACodeWithAnAmbiguousCharacterRedeems` mints an invite, finds the spelling of the printed code
that differs only in `1`/`I`/`L`, posts that spelling, and asserts `200` — not `410`.

  ```go
  func (d *DMs) Register(mux *server.Mux)
  func DMChannelID(users []id.ID) id.ID   // deterministic for exactly two users
  ```

  the `channel_members` table, `POST /v1/dms`, `GET /v1/dms`,
  `GET /v1/channels/{id}/members`, `PUT|DELETE /v1/channels/{id}/members/{user_id}`, and the

**Idempotence, recorded as P2-D31.** interfaces.md requires "a DM between the same two users is
idempotent and returns the existing channel", while R24/D2 makes every protocol-visible id — channel
ids included — 16 bytes from `crypto/rand`. This task deviates from R24 for the 1:1 case only, with
the reason and the ordering constraint in the deviation table at the top of this file. A random
`channel_id` cannot be idempotent without a round trip, so a 1:1 DM's id is **derived**:
`DMChannelID` sorts the two 16-byte user ids and returns the first 16 bytes of
`SHA-256("dilla dm v1" || lo || hi)`. It is not a secret — it is a name, and both clients compute
the same one, which is also what makes `dilla_binding.target_id` agree without a round trip
(protocol/01: `target_id` is "`dm_id` for DMs"). A group DM (3 or more users) gets a random id,
because its membership is mutable and a derived id would change when a member left.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `internal/api/dms_test.go`:

```go
package api_test

import (
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/fxamacker/cbor/v2"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/api"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/store"
)

func dmEnv(t *testing.T) (*env, *recordingDS) {
	t.Helper()
	e := newEnv(t)
	dsvc := &recordingDS{}
	inst, err := e.Repo.GetInstance(t.Context())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInstance: %v", err)
	}
	api.NewDMs(e.Repo, dsvc, e.Clk, inst.ID, 10, slog.New(slog.DiscardHandler)).Register(e.Mux)
	return e, dsvc
}

func openDM(t *testing.T, e *env, tok string, with ...id.ID) (id.ID, int) {
	t.Helper()
	status, body := e.Do(http.MethodPost, "/v1/dms", tok, []any{with})
	if status != http.StatusOK && status != http.StatusCreated {
		return id.ID{}, status
	}
	var out []cbor.RawMessage
	mustUnmarshalBody(t, body, &out)
	var ch id.ID
	mustUnmarshal(t, out[0], &ch)
	return ch, status
}

func TestADMIsIdempotent(t *testing.T) {
	e, _ := dmEnv(t)
	alice, aliceTok := e.NewUser("alice")
	bob, bobTok := e.NewUser("bob")

	first, status := openDM(t, e, aliceTok, bob)
	if status != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("first POST /v1/dms = %d", status)
	}
	second, status := openDM(t, e, aliceTok, bob)
	if status != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("second POST /v1/dms = %d, want 200", status)
	}
	if first != second {
		t.Fatalf("second DM = %x, want %x", second, first)
	}
	// Bob opening it from his side lands on the same channel.
	fromBob, status := openDM(t, e, bobTok, alice)
	if status != http.StatusOK || fromBob != first {
		t.Fatalf("bob's DM = %x (%d), want %x", fromBob, status, first)
	}
	if first != api.DMChannelID([]id.ID{alice, bob}) {
		t.Fatalf("channel id is not the derived DM id")
	}
	row, err := e.Repo.GetChannel(t.Context(), first)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetChannel: %v", err)
	}
	if row.Kind != api.ChannelDM || row.CommunityID != nil || row.Mode != api.ModeE2EE ||
		row.Visibility != api.VisPrivate {
		t.Fatalf("DM channel = %+v", row)
	}
}

func TestAGroupDMCapsAtItsConfiguredSize(t *testing.T) {
	e, _ := dmEnv(t) // maxGroupDM = 10
	_, tok := e.NewUser("alice")
	others := make([]id.ID, 0, 12)
	for i := range 12 {
		u, _ := e.NewUser(fmt.Sprintf("u%02d", i))
		others = append(others, u)
	}
	if _, status := openDM(t, e, tok, others[:9]...); status != http.StatusCreated {
		t.Fatalf("10-member group DM = %d", status)
	}
	_, status := openDM(t, e, tok, others...)
	if status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatalf("13-member group DM = %d, want 400", status)
	}
	if _, status := openDM(t, e, tok); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatal("a DM with no recipient was accepted")
	}
	if _, status := openDM(t, e, tok, userOf(t, e, tok)); status != http.StatusBadRequest {
		t.Fatal("a DM with yourself was accepted")
	}
}

func TestEveryRecipientDeviceIsAddedAndGetsAWelcome(t *testing.T) {
	e, dsvc := dmEnv(t)
	_, aliceTok := e.NewUser("alice")
	bob, _ := e.NewUser("bob")
	// Bob has three devices, each with an available KeyPackage.
	bobDevices := seedDevices(t, e, bob, 3)
	for _, d := range bobDevices {
		seedKeyPackage(t, e, d)
	}
	ch, _ := openDM(t, e, aliceTok, bob)

	// The DM channel exists before the group does: the creating client registers
	// the MLS group, and the instance then adds every eligible device.
	group := seedTextGroup(t, e, ch, id.ID{})
	if err := api.SyncGroupMembers(t.Context(), e.Repo, dsvc, mustChannel(t, e, ch), e.Clk.Now().Unix()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SyncGroupMembers: %v", err)
	}
	if len(dsvc.Adds) != len(bobDevices) {
		t.Fatalf("Adds = %d, want %d", len(dsvc.Adds), len(bobDevices))
	}
	for _, a := range dsvc.Adds {
		if a.Group != group {
			t.Fatalf("Add on the wrong group: %x", a.Group)
		}
		if !slices.Contains(bobDevices, a.Device) {
			t.Fatalf("Add for a device that is not bob's: %x", a.Device)
		}
	}
	// A device with no KeyPackage is not proposed; the delivery service would
	// refuse it, and proposing it would burn an election round.
	quiet := seedDevices(t, e, bob, 1)[0]
	dsvc.Adds = nil
	if err := api.SyncGroupMembers(t.Context(), e.Repo, dsvc, mustChannel(t, e, ch), e.Clk.Now().Unix()); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SyncGroupMembers: %v", err)
	}
	for _, a := range dsvc.Adds {
		if a.Device == quiet {
			t.Fatal("a device with no KeyPackage was proposed")
		}
	}
}

func TestTheBindingTargetIsTheDMAndTheCommunityIsNull(t *testing.T) {
	e, _ := dmEnv(t)
	_, aliceTok := e.NewUser("alice")
	bob, _ := e.NewUser("bob")
	ch, _ := openDM(t, e, aliceTok, bob)
	row, err := e.Repo.GetChannel(t.Context(), ch)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetChannel: %v", err)
	}
	inst, err := e.Repo.GetInstance(t.Context())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("GetInstance: %v", err)
	}
	b := api.ExpectedBinding(inst.ID, row)
	if b.TargetID != ch {
		t.Fatalf("binding target_id = %x, want %x", b.TargetID, ch)
	}
	if b.CommunityID != nil {
		t.Fatalf("binding community_id = %x, want null", *b.CommunityID)
	}
	if b.Kind != api.GroupText {
		t.Fatalf("binding kind = %d, want 0", b.Kind)
	}
	// protocol/01 rule 1: instance_id is the first thing the delivery service
	// compares, so a zero one makes the whole check vacuous.
	if b.InstanceID != inst.ID {
		t.Fatalf("binding instance_id = %x, want %x", b.InstanceID, inst.ID)
	}
	if b.InstanceID == (id.ID{}) {
		t.Fatal("binding instance_id is the zero id")
	}
	if b.V != 1 || b.E2EEVersion != 1 || b.MediaVersion != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("binding versions = (v %d, e2ee %d, media %d), want (1, 1, 0)",
			b.V, b.E2EEVersion, b.MediaVersion)
	}
}

func TestABlockedUserCannotOpenADM(t *testing.T) {
	e, _ := dmEnv(t)
	_, aliceTok := e.NewUser("alice")
	bob, _ := e.NewUser("bob")
	// A disabled account is the v1 block: interfaces.md §4.3 gives users a
	// disabled_at column and no per-user block list, so "blocked" is the account
	// state the instance actually stores.
	if err := e.Repo.SetUserDisabled(t.Context(), bob, ptr(e.Clk.Now().Unix())); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SetUserDisabled: %v", err)
	}
	_, status := openDM(t, e, aliceTok, bob)
	if status != http.StatusForbidden {
		t.Fatalf("DM to a disabled account = %d, want 403", status)
	}
}
```

```sql
-- name: PutChannelMember :exec
INSERT INTO channel_members (channel_id, user_id, added)
VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT (channel_id, user_id) DO NOTHING;

-- name: DeleteChannelMember :execrows
DELETE FROM channel_members WHERE channel_id = ? AND user_id = ?;

-- name: ListChannelMembers :many
SELECT user_id FROM channel_members WHERE channel_id = ? ORDER BY user_id;

-- name: ListChannelsForUser :many
SELECT c.id, c.community_id, c.kind, c.mode, c.visibility, c.parent_id, c.name, c.topic,
       c.position, c.settings_json, c.host_policy_version, c.slowmode_seconds, c.seq,
       c.created, c.deleted_at
FROM channels c
JOIN channel_members m ON m.channel_id = c.id

ORDER BY c.created DESC, c.id;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the handlers and the sync**

Create `internal/api/dms.go`:

```go
package api

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"slices"

	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/auth"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/clock"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/server"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/store"
)

// dmIDLabel is the domain separator for the derived 1:1 DM id. It is a name,
// not a secret: both clients compute it so that dilla_binding.target_id
// (protocol/01, "dm_id for DMs") agrees without a round trip.
const dmIDLabel = "dilla dm v1"

// DMChannelID returns the deterministic channel id of a 1:1 DM. For any other
// number of users it returns the zero id, and the caller mints a random one.
func DMChannelID(users []id.ID) id.ID {
	if len(users) != 2 {
		return id.ID{}
	}
	lo, hi := users[0], users[1]
	if bytes.Compare(lo[:], hi[:]) > 0 {
		lo, hi = hi, lo
	}
	h := sha256.New()
	h.Write([]byte(dmIDLabel))
	h.Write(lo[:])
	h.Write(hi[:])
	var out id.ID
	copy(out[:], h.Sum(nil)[:16])
	return out
}

// Binding is the dilla_binding a group bound to this channel must carry
// (protocol/01 "dilla_binding"). The delivery service compares a registration
// against it; the api package computes it so both agree in one place.
type Binding struct {
	V             uint64
	InstanceID    id.ID
	CommunityID   *id.ID
	TargetID      id.ID
	Kind          uint8
	PolicyVersion uint64
	E2EEVersion   uint64
	MediaVersion  uint64
}

// ExpectedBinding returns the text-group binding for a channel. Call groups use
// Kind = GroupCall and MediaVersion = 1; task 16 mints those.
//
// instanceID is a parameter and not a package variable because protocol/01 rule 1
// makes instance_id the FIRST field a client and the delivery service compare
// (E_BINDING). A zero instance id would make the comparison either vacuous or
// always-false, depending on which side had it, and neither failure is visible in
// a test that checks only target_id.
func ExpectedBinding(instanceID id.ID, ch store.ChannelRow) Binding {
	return Binding{
		V:             protocolBindingV,
		InstanceID:    instanceID,
		CommunityID:   ch.CommunityID,
		TargetID:      ch.ID,
		Kind:          GroupText,
		PolicyVersion: ch.HostPolicyVersion,
		E2EEVersion:   e2eeVersion,
		MediaVersion:  mediaVersionText,
	}
}

// The three fixed versions of the binding array, from protocol/07-versioning.md's
// change table: wire, e2ee and media all stand at 1, and a text group carries no
// media, so its media_version slot is 0. They are named constants rather than
// literals so that a version bump is one edit and `go vet` finds every use.
const (
	protocolBindingV  uint64 = 1
	e2eeVersion       uint64 = 1
	mediaVersionText  uint64 = 0
	mediaVersionCall  uint64 = 1
)

type DMs struct {
	repo       store.Repository
	dsvc       DS
	clk        clock.Clock
	maxGroupDM int
	log        *slog.Logger
}

func NewDMs(repo store.Repository, dsvc DS, clk clock.Clock, maxGroupDM int, log *slog.Logger) *DMs {
	return &DMs{repo: repo, dsvc: dsvc, clk: clk, maxGroupDM: maxGroupDM, log: log}
}

func (d *DMs) Register(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("POST /v1/dms", d.open)
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /v1/dms", d.list)
}

type openDMReq struct {
	_          struct{} `cbor:",toarray"`
	Recipients []id.ID
}

func (d *DMs) open(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, ok := auth.SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		server.WriteError(w, errUnauthenticated())
		return
	}
	var req openDMReq
	if err := server.DecodeBody(w, r, 64*1024, &req); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	users := append([]id.ID{s.UserID}, req.Recipients...)
	slices.SortFunc(users, func(a, b id.ID) int { return bytes.Compare(a[:], b[:]) })
	users = slices.Compact(users)
	if len(users) < 2 {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"a DM needs at least one recipient other than yourself"))
		return
	}
	if len(users) > d.maxGroupDM {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"a group DM holds at most %d members", d.maxGroupDM))
		return
	}
	for _, u := range users {
		row, err := d.repo.GetUser(r.Context(), u)
		if err != nil {
			server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeNotFound, "no such user"))
			return
		}
		if row.DisabledAt != nil || row.DeletedAt != nil {
			server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
				"that account cannot receive messages"))
			return
		}
	}

	kind := ChannelGroupDM
	chID := DMChannelID(users)
	if len(users) == 2 {
		kind = ChannelDM
		if existing, err := d.repo.GetChannel(r.Context(), chID); err == nil {
			if err := server.EncodeBody(w, http.StatusOK, []id.ID{existing.ID}); err != nil {
				d.log.Error("encode dm", "err", err)
			}
			return
		} else if !errors.Is(err, store.ErrNotFound) {
			server.WriteError(w, err)
			return
		}
	} else {
		chID = id.New()
	}

	now := d.clk.Now().Unix()
	row := store.ChannelRow{
		ID: chID, CommunityID: nil, Kind: kind, Mode: ModeE2EE, Visibility: VisPrivate,
		Name: "", Topic: "", SettingsJSON: []byte("{}"), HostPolicyVersion: 1, Created: now,
	}
	if err := d.repo.Tx(r.Context(), func(tx store.Repository) error {
		if err := tx.CreateChannel(r.Context(), row); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		for _, u := range users {
			if err := tx.PutChannelMember(r.Context(), chID, u, now); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if err := server.EncodeBody(w, http.StatusCreated, []id.ID{chID}); err != nil {
		d.log.Error("encode dm", "err", err)
	}
}

type dmResp struct {
	_         struct{} `cbor:",toarray"`
	ChannelID id.ID
	Kind      uint64
	Members   []id.ID
}

func (d *DMs) list(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, ok := auth.SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !ok {
		server.WriteError(w, errUnauthenticated())
		return
	}
	rows, err := d.repo.ListChannelsForUser(r.Context(), s.UserID)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	out := make([]dmResp, 0, len(rows))
	for _, ch := range rows {
		members, err := d.repo.ListChannelMembers(r.Context(), ch.ID)
		if err != nil {
			server.WriteError(w, err)
			return
		}
		out = append(out, dmResp{ChannelID: ch.ID, Kind: uint64(ch.Kind), Members: members})
	}
	if err := server.EncodeBody(w, http.StatusOK, out); err != nil {
		d.log.Error("encode dms", "err", err)
	}
}

// SyncGroupMembers brings the text group bound to ch in line with
// channel_members: every device of an eligible user that holds an available
// KeyPackage and is not already a live leaf is proposed for Add, and every live
// leaf whose user is no longer eligible is proposed for Remove. It is the one
// function every membership change funnels through, and it is idempotent: a
// second call with nothing changed issues nothing.
func SyncGroupMembers(ctx context.Context, repo store.Repository, dsvc DS, ch store.ChannelRow, now int64) error {
	if !TextGroupAllowed(ch) {
		return nil
	}
	groups, err := repo.GroupsForTarget(ctx, ch.ID, GroupText)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	eligible, err := repo.ListChannelMembers(ctx, ch.ID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for _, g := range groups {
		leaves, err := repo.ListMembers(ctx, g.GroupID)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		live := make(map[id.ID]bool, len(leaves))
		actionID := id.New()
		for _, m := range leaves {
			if m.RemovedEpoch != nil {
				continue
			}
			live[m.DeviceID] = true
			if !slices.Contains(eligible, m.UserID) {
				if err := dsvc.ProposeRemove(ctx, g.GroupID, m.LeafIndex, actionID); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		}
		var want []id.ID
		for _, u := range eligible {
			devices, err := repo.ListDevicesByUser(ctx, u)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			for _, dev := range devices {
				if dev.RevokedAt != nil || dev.QuarantinedAt != nil || live[dev.ID] {
					continue
				}
				n, err := repo.CountKeyPackages(ctx, dev.ID, now)
				if err != nil {
					return err
				}
				if n == 0 {
					// The delivery service would refuse the Add and the round
					// would be wasted; the device is picked up on its next
					// KeyPackage publication.
					continue
				}
				want = append(want, dev.ID)
			}
		}
		if len(want) > 0 {
			if err := dsvc.ProposeAddBatch(ctx, g.GroupID, want); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
	}
	return nil
}
```

`now` is the caller's `clk.Now().Unix()`; `CountKeyPackages` takes it so an expired KeyPackage is
not counted as available. Every caller in this plan passes `clk.Now().Unix()`, and the test above
calls `api.SyncGroupMembers(t.Context(), e.Repo, dsvc, mustChannel(t, e, ch), e.Clk.Now().Unix())`.

`SyncGroupMembers` is also **never called from inside a `store.Repository.Tx`**, for the reason
given on `RemoveUserFromCommunityGroups` in task 4: it calls the delivery service, which writes
through the same single-connection write pool.

**The three §5.2 channel-member routes land here**, because `channel_members` is this task's table
and they are the only §5.2 routes with no owning task. Add to `internal/api/channels.go`:

```go
// RegisterMembers registers the three channel-membership routes of §5.2. They are
// separate from Register because channel_members is task 6's table: a build of
// tasks 1-5 alone must not advertise a route with no storage behind it.
func (c *Channels) RegisterMembers(mux *server.Mux) {
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /v1/channels/{id}/members", c.members)
	mux.HandleFunc("PUT /v1/channels/{id}/members/{user_id}", c.addMember)
	mux.HandleFunc("DELETE /v1/channels/{id}/members/{user_id}", c.removeMember)
}

// members lists the channel's materialised membership. On a community channel
// that is the set task 7 derives from the resolver; on a group DM it is the
// participant list, which is the only place it is stored.
func (c *Channels) members(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	row, _, err := c.visible(r)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	ids, err := c.repo.ListChannelMembers(r.Context(), row.ID)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	out := make([][]id.ID, 0, len(ids))
	for _, u := range ids {
		out = append(out, []id.ID{u})
	}
	if err := server.EncodeBody(w, http.StatusOK, out); err != nil {
		c.log.Error("encode channel members", "err", err)
	}
}

// addMember adds one participant to a GROUP DM. A community channel's membership
// is derived from the permission resolver and is never written by hand, so the
// route refuses it: a hand-written row would be overwritten by the next
// MaterialiseChannelMembers pass and the caller would never know.
func (c *Channels) addMember(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	row, s, err := c.visible(r)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if row.Kind != ChannelGroupDM {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"only a group DM's membership is set directly; a channel's follows its permissions"))
		return
	}
	target, err := server.PathID(r, "user_id")
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	existing, err := c.repo.ListChannelMembers(r.Context(), row.ID)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	if !slices.Contains(existing, s.UserID) {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeNotFound, "no such object"))
		return
	}
	if len(existing) >= c.maxGroupDM {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeInvalidRequest,
			"a group DM holds at most %d members", c.maxGroupDM))
		return
	}
	u, err := c.repo.GetUser(r.Context(), target)
	if err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeNotFound, "no such user"))
		return
	}
	if u.DisabledAt != nil || u.DeletedAt != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, server.Errorf(server.CodeForbidden,
			"that account cannot receive messages"))
		return
	}
	now := c.clk.Now().Unix()
	if err := c.repo.PutChannelMember(r.Context(), row.ID, target, now); err != nil {
		server.WriteError(w, err)
		return
	}
	// After the write, never inside a transaction: SyncGroupMembers calls the
	// delivery service. See task 4's note on the single-writer pool.
	if err := SyncGroupMembers(r.Context(), c.repo, c.dsvc, row, now); err != nil {
		c.log.Error("sync group members after add", "channel", row.ID, "err", err)
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

// removeMember is the mirror image: DeleteChannelMember, then SyncGroupMembers,
// which issues the Removes for every leaf the departing user held. A participant
// may always remove themselves; removing someone else needs the same group-DM
// membership, because a group DM has no roles.
func (c *Channels) removeMember(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { /* … as above, with DeleteChannelMember … */ }
```

```go
package ds_test

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/ds"
	"github.com/jonasthim/dilla/internal/id"
)

func TestPlanBatchesNeverExceedsTheCommitCap(t *testing.T) {
	for _, n := range []int{0, 1, 255, 256, 257, 512, 1000} {
		devices := make([]id.ID, n)
		for i := range devices {
			devices[i] = id.New()
		}
		plan := ds.PlanBatches(devices, 256)
		var total int
		for i, b := range plan.Batches {
			if len(b) == 0 {
				t.Fatalf("n=%d: batch %d is empty", n, i)
			}
			if len(b) > 256 {
				t.Fatalf("n=%d: batch %d holds %d devices", n, i, len(b))
			}
			total += len(b)
		}
		if total != n {
			t.Fatalf("n=%d: batches hold %d devices", n, total)
		}
		want := (n + 255) / 256
		if len(plan.Batches) != want {
			t.Fatalf("n=%d: %d batches, want %d", n, len(plan.Batches), want)
		}
	}
}

func TestPlanBatchesIsOrderPreservingAndDeduplicated(t *testing.T) {
	a, b := id.New(), id.New()
	plan := ds.PlanBatches([]id.ID{a, b, a, b, a}, 2)
	if len(plan.Batches) != 1 || len(plan.Batches[0]) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("plan = %+v, want one batch of two", plan)
	}
	if plan.Batches[0][0] != a || plan.Batches[0][1] != b {
		t.Fatal("PlanBatches reordered the devices")
	}
}
```
